○0000004446○Now then...

○0000004447○What will I change into?
●0000004447●

○0000004449○A single old tome was excavated after evacuating from the Key.
●0000004449●

○0000004450○It was translated by the hands of a member of the Guardian Far East Branch 11th Unit (code name is a number withheld and determined via remnant memories)...
●0000004450●

○0000004451○...who possesses "psychometric" powers―the ability to touch and read the remnant memories of an object―Awaji.
●0000004451●

○0000004452○We assume that the memories left behind in this book are those left on the last page.
●0000004452●

○0000004453○Any relevant pages beforehand are unfortunately either extremely lacking in data to decipher them, or lack the linguistic data to conduct proper research, making translating them a lost cause.
●0000004453●

○0000004454○The following four lines were written in the aforementioned last page.
●0000004454●

○0000004455○"The Destruction incurred by Pleiades is not borne of the evolution of human growth or prosperity, nor is it borne of their hearts."
●0000004455●

○0000004456○"We know not if we have walked the right path."
●0000004456●

○0000004457○"However, should the people of this planet take upon its judgment in the distant future..."
●0000004457●

○0000004458○"I only pray that we will have matured in the proper way."
●0000004458●

○0000004459○According to Awaji, the entity referred to as "Pleiades" is one and the same as the entity we refer to as the "Key." (It is unknown why it was read and translated in that way.)
●0000004459●

○0000004460○These four lines are an important clue regarding the system in which this "Destruction" operates.
●0000004460●

○0000004461○However, as for "proper maturity," out of fear for those who will read this book one day, we have decided to omit the writer's mistaken interpretation.
●0000004461●

○0000004462○We were all mistaken.
●0000004462●

○0000004463○The following is nothing more than a study on said writer. 
●0000004463●

○0000004464○It is clear that this book implies that there was a "previous humanity," a different civilization, long before we humans existed.
●0000004464●

○0000004465○It has long since been unclear why we knew about the Key and why we split off into two factions called "Gaia" and "Guardian" to persist in a war that has lasted longer than recorded history.
●0000004465●

○0000004466○If we assume all of humanity ended up destroyed, then it would naturally wipe out any and all records, as well.
●0000004466●

○0000004467○It should mean that no memories of prior Destruction would be left, and there would be no way for us to know about Destruction itself.
●0000004467●

○0000004468○Was it not because they kept records of Destruction denoted in this book somewhere?
●0000004468●

○0000004469○Was it not an act done by the previous humanity?
●0000004469●

○0000004470○Judging from the facilities of this shelter, there is no doubt that the previous humanity possessed a civilization much more advanced than our own.
●0000004470●

○0000004471○In which case, we must be the bearers of this message.
●0000004471●

○0000004472○We must pray that our "descendants" give birth to even better memories.
●0000004472●

○0000004474○How many days have passed?
●0000004474●

○0000004475○I started keeping a diary halfway through, but I ended up leaving a gap, so I don't know anymore.
●0000004475●

○0000004476○I feel like it's been a long time.
